     V പാലക്കാട് ജില്ല
          1. ശൈവ വെള്ളാള (ചേരകല വെള്ളാള, കാര്‍കാര്‍ത്ത വെള്ളാള, ചോഴിയ
       വെള്ളാള, പിള്ളൈ)
വിശദീകരണം:-           ഈ പട്ടികയില്‍ മലബാര്‍ ജില്ല എന്നതു കൊണ്ട് അര്‍ത്ഥമാക്കുന്നത്
1956-ലെ സ്റ്റേറ്റ് റീ ഓര്‍ഗനൈസേഷന്‍ ആക്ടിലെ സെക്ഷന്‍ 5-ല്‍ സബ് സെക്ഷന്‍ 2-ല്‍
പരാമര്‍ശിച്ചിട്ടുള്ള ഡിസ്ട്രിക്റ്റാണ് .

                                  Part II
                            GENERAL CONDITIONS

1.     Candidates must comply with the following conditions unless specifically
       exempted in Part I of this notification relating to each post:
       (a)    Must be Citizens of India
                      OR
       (b)    Subjects of Nepal
                      OR
       (c)    Subjects of Bhutan
                      OR
       (d)    Tibetans who have migrated to India before 1 st January, 1962 with
              the intention of permanently settling in India.
                      OR
       (e)    Persons of Indian origin who have migrated from Pakistan, Burma,
              Srilanka and East African Countries of Kenya, Uganda and United
              Tanzanian Republic (erstwhile Tankanika and Sansibar) with the
              intention of permanently settling in India.
       Note:- Persons mentioned in (b), (c), (d) and (e) above shall obtain
             certificate of eligibility from Government of India. They will be
             allowed to sit for the examination and appear for the interview and
             in case they are appointed to any post, their appointment will be
             only provisional, till they produce the certificate of eligibility.
     (f)     Shall be of good character.
     (g)     Unless otherwise exempted by the Government, a candidate if
             male, shall not have more than one wife living and if female shall
             not have married a person who has a wife living.
2.   Special Concessions in Upper age limits.
     Note:- The following concessions in upper age limits are available subject
             to the conditions that the maximum age limit shall in no case
             exceed 50 (fifty) years.


     (i)     The upper age limit prescribed shall be raised by five years in the
             case of candidates belonging to any of the Scheduled Castes or
             adult members of such Caste and their children when such adult
             members are converted to other religion or Scheduled Tribes and
             by three years in the case of candidates belonging to any of the
             Other Backward Classes. [Rule 10(c) of Part II, KS&SSR, G.O.
             (Ms) No. 170/66/PD dated 28.04.1966, G.O.(Ms) No. 647/61/PD
             dated 09.08.1961]
     (ii)    The maximum age limit laid down for appointment to any post
             shall not apply in the case of candidates belonging to any of the
             Scheduled Caste or adult members of such castes and their children
             when such adult members are converted to other religions or
             Scheduled Tribes, if the qualification prescribed for the post is
             lower than a degree of recognised University and if such candidates
             possess an educational qualification higher than that prescribed for
             the post, they are otherwise qualified for appointment. [Proviso (i)
             to Rule 10(c) of Part II, KS&SSR]
     (iii)   The maximum age limit shall not apply to the appointment of
       candidates belonging to any of the Scheduled Castes or adult
       members of such caste and their children when such adult members
       are converted to other religions or Scheduled Tribes if the
       minimum qualification prescribed for the post is a degree and if the
       candidates hold a degree which is not lower than the qualification
       prescribed for the post provided they are otherwise qualified for
       appointment. [Proviso (ii) to Rule 10(c) of Part II, KS&SSR]
Note:- The words educational qualification mentioned in paras 2(ii) and
       2(iii) refers to technical qualification in the case of technical posts
       and general educational qualification in the case of non-technical
       posts.
(iv)   Where age alone stands in the way of recruitment of candidates
       belonging to any of the Scheduled Caste/Scheduled Tribes or adult
       members of the Scheduled Castes converted to other religions and
       their children of Scheduled Tribes converted to other religions, the
       upper age limit may be relaxed to fill up the quota reserved for
       them. [Rule 10(d) of Part II, KS&SSR]
(v)    Relaxation of age will be allowed to all provisional employees of
       the Kerala Government (whether they continue in service or
       relieved from service) who have been appointed under Rule 9(a)(i)
       of Part II of the Kerala State and Subordinate Service Rules, 1958
       to the extent of their provisional service provided they possess a
       minimum service of one year in the provisional appointment. This
       concession will be extended to the provisional employees
       appointed to Work Establishment posts and Part-time posts. Once
       they get appointment in the regular basis, the concession will not
       be available to further appointment to any other posts. For selection
       to posts in the service of the Local Authorities, upper age limit will
       be relaxed in favour of those candidates who have worked for a
       minimum period of one year provisionally in the service of the
       Local Authorities to the extent of their provisional service up to a
       maximum of five years provided they were within the prescribed
       age limit at the time of their initial provisional appointment. The
       above concession in age will not be applicable to persons holding
       regular appointments under the Local Authorities for further
       appointments. Candidates who claim this relaxation have to
       produce a certificate showing the period of provisional service put
       in by them as and when required by the Commission. It should be
       specifically stated in the certificate that they were not holding
       regular appointments under the Local Authorities. [G.O.(Ms) No.
       389/66/PD dated 04.10.1966, G.O.(Ms) No. 245/69/PD dated
       13.08.1969, G.O.(P) No. 437/72/PD dated 20.11.1972, G.O.(Ms)
       No. 224/73/PD dated 17.08.1973, G.O.(P) No. 182/74/PD dated
       26.07.1974]
(vi)   The maximum age limit prescribed for appointment to a post will
       be relaxed in the case of Ex-servicemen to the extent of the period
       of service put in by them in the Defence Forces and the period of
       unemployment on discharge up to a maximum of five years. The
       relaxation in upper age limit allowed to Ex- servicemen will be
       extended to Ex-General Reserve Engineer Force Personnel,
       Reservists, Retired Defence Service Personnel, those who have
       rendered service in the erstwhile Travancore-Cochin Labour Units,
       N.C.C Cadet Instructors who have been retrenched after a specified
       period of service, the retrenched whole time N.C.C Instructors who
       have put in minimum period of service of six months before
       retrenchment and Disembodied Territorial Army Personnel.
       Discharge certificate and the Certificate obtained from the District
       Sainik Welfare Officer, in original, shall be produced in proof of
       service in the defence forces and the period of unemployment
       respectively as and when required by the Commission. [G.O.(P)
       No. 109/66/PD dated 21.03.1966, G.O.(P) No. 309/70/PD dated
       29.08.1970, G.O.(Ms) No. 110/72/PD dated 16.03.1972, G.O.(Ms)
        No. 327/66/PD dated 10.08.1966, G.O.(Ms) No. 2/68/PD dated
        05.01.1968, G.O.(P) No. 8/73/PD dated 04.01.1973, G.O.(Ms) No.
        272/69/PD dated 11.09.1969, G.O.(P) No. 479/66/PD dated
        08.12.1966, G.O.(Ms) No. 962/61/PD dated 31.10.1961]
(vii)   Differently Abled candidates belongs to Blindness and Low Vision,
        Deaf and hard of hearing, Locomotor disability including Cerebral
        Palsy, Leprosy cured, Dwarfism, Acid attack victims and Muscular
        Dystrophy, Autism, Intellectual disability, Specific learning
        disability and Mental illness, Multiple disabilities may also apply if
        they satisfy all the prescribed qualifications except age. The
        prescribed upper age limit will be enhanced for such candidates.
        Age concession upto 15 years will be granted to candidates
        belonging to Blindness and Low Vision and Deaf and hard of
        hearing categories as per G.O.(Ms) No. 30/78/GAD dated
        16.01.1978 and 10 years to candidates belonging to Locomotor
        disability including Cerebral Palsy, Leprosy cured, Dwarfism, Acid
        attack victims and Muscular Dystrophy, Autism, Intellectual
        disability, Specific learning disability and Mental illness as per
        G.O.(P) No. 158/73/PD dated 29.05.1973. In the case of candidates
        having Multiple disabilities, age concession upto 15 years will be
        granted, if one of their disability includes Blindness, Low Vision or
        Deaf and hard of hearing and age concession upto 10 years will be
        granted to other multiple disabled candidates. They shall produce a
        certificate from the competent authorities in the format prescribed
        in Appendix I, II, III (whichever is applicable) to prove their
        disability as and when required by the Commission.
Note:- The term Differently Abled used in this notification means ‘persons
        with benchmark disabilities’ as mentioned in Section 2(r) and 2(s)
        of ‘The Rights of Persons with Disabilities Act, 2016’ and having
        not less than 40% disability. The definition of various categories of
        persons with benchmark disabilities eligible for reservation in
         appointments will be as given in the Schedule to clause (zc) of
         Section 2 of the aforementioned Act.
(viii)   The upper age limit will be relaxed in the case of ex-employees of
         the Bharat Sevak Samaj engaged for the execution of any scheme
         financed by the State or Central Government to the extent of their
         service in the Samaj, provided they are otherwise qualified and
         provided further that (1) they shall not have passed 40 years of age
         on the first day of January of the year in which applications are
         invited and (2) such candidates shall produce, along with their
         application, a certificate obtained from the Pradesh Chairman of
         the Samaj stating that they had been appointed for the execution of
         the scheme mentioned above, as and when required by the
         Commission. [G.O.(Ms) No. 302/71/PD dated 14.09.1971]
(ix)     The inmates as well as Ex-inmates of After Care Homes,
         Orphanages, Rescue Homes and Ex-inmates of Juvenile Homes
         and Special Homes which are recognised by Government or are
         receiving grant from Government shall be allowed age relaxation
         upto 10 (ten) years, for appointment to posts in the various services
         of the State if they are otherwise qualified. Candidates who are
         eligible for concession shall note the above details in their profile
         at the time of making the applications and shall produce a
         certificate obtained from the competent authority to prove that they
         belong to the category concerned, as and when required by the
         Commission. [G.O.(Ms) No. 266/72/PD dated 07.07.1972, G.O.
         (Ms)   No.   307/79/GAD      dated     23.05.1979,   G.O.(Ms)    No.
         30/94/P&ARD dated 06.09.1994]
(x)      The upper age limit shall be relaxed in the case of Labourers of the
         Indo-Swiss Project to the extent of their Service in the project, if
         they are otherwise qualified. They shall produce certificates
         showing their service (with the date of commencement and
         termination) in the project obtained from the Head of the project,
         when required by the Commission.
(xi)     Ex-Home Guards Volunteers who have completed a tenure of three
         years in the organisation and who had undergone training during
         the period satisfactorily will be allowed age relaxation for the
         entire period of their service in the Home Guards Organisation for
         all appointments in Public Service. They will also be allowed age
         relaxation for the period of their unemployment subject to a
         maximum period of seven years of such appointments. Such
         candidates shall obtain proper certificates showing the period of
         their service in the Home Guards Organisation and the period of
         unemployment from the Superintendent of Police/ Commissioner
         of Police of the District concerned and shall produce the same as
         and when required by the Commission. [Rule 10(g) of Part II of
         KS&SSR, G.O.(Rt) No. 509/79/Home dated 05.03.1979]
(xii)    The crucial date for determining the upper age limit of trained
         apprentice to all appointments in Non-Gazetted/ Class III and Class
         IV/Posts in Government and to equivalent posts in Government
         owned/ Controlled Companies, Boards, Corporations will be
         determined   by   deducting    from   his   age   the   period   of
         apprenticeship /training undergone by him as prescribed under the
         Apprentice Act, 1961, subject to the condition that the
         apprenticeship/ training in the same Organisation or in the same
         line of work/trade. [G.O.(Ms) No. 107/81/LBR dated 08.12.1981]
(xiii)   The upper age limit will be relaxed in the case of candidates who
         have rendered service in the personal staff of Ministers/ Leader of
         Opposition/ Government Chief Whip/ Speaker/ Deputy Speaker/
         Chairman of Financial Committees of Kerala to the extent of the
         period of their said service provided they are otherwise qualified.
         They shall produce a certificate issued by competent authority
         specifying the period of their said service with the date of
         commencement and termination thereof when required by the
             Commission. This relaxation is not allowed to Government
             Servants who work in the personal staff as aforesaid. [Rule 10(h) of
             Part II of KS&SSR, G.O.(P) No. 25/93/P&ARD dated 01-04-1993
             and G.O.(P) No. 33/97/P&ARD dated 22-12-1997].
     (xiv)   The upper age limit prescribed in the Special Rules, shall be further
             raised by five years in the case of widows subject to the condition
             that the maximum upper age limit shall in no case exceed fifty
             years. (G.O.(P) No. 10/2018/P&ARD dated 29.05.2018 – SRO No.
             340/2018)
3.   Special concession regarding educational qualifications:- In case where the
     minimum educational qualification for posts other than teaching posts in
     Colleges is prescribed as First or Second Class Degree/ Diploma, it shall
     be fixed as the lowest class of such Degree/ Diploma in respect of
     candidates belonging to Scheduled Castes and Scheduled Tribes. [G.O.(P)
     No. 7/73/PD dated 04.01.1973]
4.   (a)     If in a selection Ex-serviceman and Non-Ex-serviceman who
     satisfy the other conditions regarding age and qualifications get the same
     mark, the Ex-serviceman will be given preference for recruitment [G.O.
     (Ms) No. 70/73/PD dated 02.03.1973]. But, in case, where preferential
     qualification is prescribed in the recruitment rules and a candidate having
     preferential qualification and Ex-servicemen get the same mark, then
     candidate having preferential qualification will be given preference for
     recruitment. Preference will be within the meaning of G.O.(Ms.) No.
     50/70/PD. dated 12-2-1970.
     (b)     National Apprenticeship Certificate holders will be given
     preference over National Trade Certificate holders in the matter of
     selection of candidates subject to the condition that the preference will be
     within the meaning of G.O.(Ms.) No. 50/70/PD. dated 12-2-1970 as
     amended by G.O.(Ms) No. 246/70/PD dated 21-7-1970. [Vide G.O.(Ms)
     No. 112/81/LBR dated 22-12-1981].
5.   A certain percentage of vacancies, from among those reported for General
     recruitment, will be apportioned for Differently Abled candidates in the
     posts identified by the State Government as suitable for them for
     discharging the duties, as per ‘The Rights of Persons with Disabilities
     Act’.
6.   (a)     Extra marks will be awarded to meritorious Sportsmen who
     possess the requisite qualifications and are within age, in selections to
     Class III and Class IV posts as per G.O. (Ms). No. 21/78/GAD dated 11-
     01-1978. Accordingly the Sportsmen who are eligible for weightage marks
     shall state the same in the relevant column in their profile at the time of
     making the application and shall produce the relevant certificate when
     required by the Commission. Candidates shall produce the Sports
     Certificate issued by the authorities mentioned in para 5 of the above
     Government Order. As regards the Certificate issued by the Amateur
     Sports Organisation affiliated to any of the National Sports Association
     mentioned in Appendix VII of the Kerala Service Rules Vol. 1 should bear
     the Countersignature of the Secretary, Kerala Sports Council. Certificates
     without the Countersignature of the Secretary, Kerala Sports Council will
     not be considered for awarding weightage marks.
     Note:- The sports/games items included in G.O.(Ms) No. 21/78/GAD
             dated 11.01.1978, G.O.(Ms) No. 129/2012/GAD dated 30.05.2012,
             G.O.(Ms) No. 88/2020/GAD dated 04.05.2020, G.O(Ms) No.
             108/2024/GAD dated 30/08/2024 are eligible for granting extra
             marks.
      (b)    Ex-servicemen receiving pensionary benefits from the Defence
      Forces will be awarded weightage marks in accordance with their period
      of service, on the basis of the claim made in their application and
      production of original documents proving the same. Detailed information
      regarding this is available on the official website of the Kerala Public
      Service Commission.
      (c)    N.C.C. Cadets who have obtained 'A', 'B' and 'C' certificates will be
      awarded weightage marks at the rate of 2%, 3% and 5% respectively for
      selection to the uniformed forces in the Departments of Police, Jail,
      Excise, Forest, Fire and Rescue Services, on the basis of the claim made
      in their application and production of original documents proving the
      same [As per G.O(Ms) No. 112/2007/H.Edn. Dated 21.08.2007].
      Detailed information regarding this is available on the official website of
      the Kerala Public Service Commission.
      (d)   Academic weightage marks will be awarded to candidates for
      selection to the posts of Teachers in Higher Secondary Schools,
      Vocational Higher Secondary Schools, Polytechnic Colleges, and
      Colleges (General & Professional) except those in the Department of
      Medical Education, on the basis of the claim made in their application
      and production of original documents proving the same. Detailed
      information regarding this is available on the official website of the
      Kerala Public Service Commission.
      (e)   For selections to the posts of Uniformed Forces, where weightage
      marks are currently awarded for N.C.C. Certificates, the students who
      successfully complete the Student Police Cadet Scheme will be awarded
      weightage marks, on the basis of the claim made in their application and
      production of original documents proving the same, as per G.O(Ms) No.
      6/2025/P&ARD dated 01.03.2025. Detailed information regarding this is
      available on the official website of the Kerala Public Service
      Commission.
     (f) Weightage/Grace marks shall be awarded for selections in state service,
     and for selections to institutions entrusted to the Kerala Public Service
     Commission as additional function, on the basis of provisions, if any
     specified in the Recruitment Rules concerned.
7.   (a). While creating profile through ‘One Time Registration’ facility,
     candidates shall upload their photograph taken after attaining the age of 18
     years and taken within a period of six months. The name of the candidate
     and the date of taking the photograph should be clearly printed at the
     bottom portion of the photograph. The photograph uploaded in the profile
     of the already registered candidate shall be one taken within 10 years.
     Other Instructions regarding uploading of photograph are as follows:
            (1) The photo uploaded should be in Colour/Black & White. The
            face and shoulder of the candidate should be clearly visible.
            (2) Images should have 200 pixels height, 150 pixels width and
            with a file size of not exceeding 30 kb, in JPG format.
            (3) The background of the photograph should be white/light
            coloured.
            (4) Face should be centrally focussed.
            (5) The eyes should be open and vivid.
            (6) Photograph wearing cap, goggles (except cap, veil worn in
            adherence to religious custom), photographs showing a portion of
            the face, photo with unclear face shall not be accepted.
     The Photograph uploaded by the candidate as per the above conditions will
     be valid for 10 years from the date of taking the photograph.
     (b).   The instructions for uploading signature at the time of One Time
     Registration is given below.
     (1) Images should have 150 pixel width, 100 pixel height in JPG format.
     (2) Scanned image of the signature should not exceed a file size of 30 kb
     (3) Uploaded image of the signature should be clear, vivid and complete.
     Note:- (1) The applications of those candidates who uploaded blank image
            or unclear or incomplete image of their signature or uploading the
            signature of any other candidates by mistake, instead of their
            signature provided in the relevant column of their profile will be
            rejected.
            (2) Candidates who can put their signature should upload the
            scanned image of their signature in the profile.
            (3) In the case of blind candidates, the image of their thumb
            impression should be uploaded instead of signature.
8.   (i)    Where the number of qualified applicants is unduly large, having
     regard to the number of vacancies, the Commission may restrict the
     number of candidates to be called for final stage of selection like oral test
     (interview) to such extent as they may decide on the basis of
     qualification/marks/written test/ practical test or on any other basis.
     (ii)    As per Rule 2A of Kerala Public Service Commission Rules of
     Procedure, the Commission shall have the power to conduct the
     preliminary screening test and to short list candidates who could be
     permitted to appear for the Rule 3 examination. The marks secured by the
     candidate in the preliminary screening test will not be counted for the final
     ranking. The Commission shall have the liberty to fix the different cut off
     marks for various reserved categories in the preliminary screening test to
     ensure their representation among the candidates eligible to appear for the
     examination prescribed under Rule 3. Relaxation in marks given at the
     preliminary stage will not have any impact in determining the final
     ranking.

     (iii)   In cases where the Kerala Public Service Commission finds it
     impractical to conduct the examination at one phase due to the large
     number of applicants for any selection, the Commission shall have the
     authority to conduct the examination at various phases, and to standardize
     the marks.

     (iv) In case an interview is conducted by the Commission for selection to
     any post, candidates who secured at least 20% marks in the interview will
     only be considered for inclusion in the Ranked List.

9.   Candidates may be required to present themselves for an interview or a
     practical/written test or for both or other tests at the time and place to be
     specified. They must bear their own travelling expenses.
     Note:- The unemployed candidates who belongs to SC/ST (and converts
             from among them including Kudumbis) who are called for
             interview/written test/ practical test/physical efficiency test and
             other tests conducted by the P.S.C are eligible for T.A. The T.A for
             to and fro journey is limited to that of actual bus fares/II Class train
             fare. The eligible candidate should obtain necessary attendance
             certificate from the Chief Superintendent/Nominee of the
             Commission on the day of the test/interview itself. Belated request
             for the issuance of attendance certificate will not be entertained.
             The candidates claiming T.A. should produce the attendance
             certificate thus obtained before the concerned officer of the SC/ST
             Development Department along with the declaration stating that
             she/he is not employed elsewhere. [G.O.(Ms) No. 140/78/DD dated
             20.09.1978, G.O. (Rt.) No. 7/83/HWD dated 06.01.1983, G.O.
             (Ms) No. 66/85/SCSTDD dated 09.12.1985 and G.O. (Ms) No.
             1/91/SC/ST/DD dated 02.01.1991]
10.   Selected candidates shall produce before the appointing authority, before
      they are admitted to duty, necessary medical certificate in the prescribed
      form and the documents in original in proof of age, qualifications,
      experience etc. together with a proforma furnishing details enabling the
      verification of character and antecedents of the candidates. [G.O.(P) No.
      20/2011/P&ARD dated 30.06.2011, G.O.(P) No. 79/2009/Home dated
      05.06.2009]

12.   Mode of sending applications:
      Candidates shall submit the applications online through their profile after
      completing the ‘One Time Registration’ process by strictly following the
      instructions given in the official website of the Commission viz.
      www.keralapsc.gov.in. Candidates who have already registered can apply
      by login to their profile using their User-ID and password. The application
      number    and       the   User   ID   shall   be   quoted   in   all   further
      correspondence/enquiries with the Commission.
13.   The following documents shall be produced by the applicants as and when
      required by the Commission:
      (a) Proof of age:
      Educational records like the S.S.L.C book or its equivalent, true copy of
      the relevant extract of the School Admission Register, Transfer certificate,
      in original, will only be accepted as proof of age in the case of candidates
      who have studied in a school or college or any other educational institution
      under the Government. [G.O.(P) No. 344/65/PD dated 27.03.1965, G.O.
      (P) No. 260/75/PD dated 03.11.1975]
             In the case of Ex-servicemen, the relevant entries in the discharge
      certificates will be accepted in proof of age, if they are unable to produce
      any of the certificates mentioned above. [G.O.(P) No. 58/74/PD dated
      15.03.1974]
             In the case of candidates who have not studied in any educational
      institution, the following will be accepted in proof of age:- [G.O.(P) No.
      344/65/PD dated 27.03.1965]
             1. True Copy of horoscope attested by a Gazetted Officer.
             2. True Copy of certificate issued from Muslim Juma-Ath attested
             by a Gazetted Officer.
          3. Relevant portion of the Baptism Register attested by the Parish
Priest.
          4. Authenticated extract from the Birth Register.
          5. Certificate of Birth issued by the Registrar of Birth and Death
          specifying name and address of the applicant.
          In the case of persons of Indian Origin born in Sri Lanka, the
certificate issued by the Department of Births and Deaths of the
Government of Sri Lanka will be accepted as sufficient proof of age. [G.O.
(Ms) No. 8/77/LBR dated 25.01.1977]
(b) Proof of Educational Qualifications:
Only the following will be accepted as proof of educational qualifications:
(i)       In the case of Graduate or Post-Graduate degrees, either the Degree
          or Provisional Certificate obtained from the University concerned.
          (Mark list will not be accepted unless it shows the Class or
          Division in which the candidate has been placed on in passing the
          degree examination).
(ii)      In the case of Diploma or Certificate holders either Diploma or
          Certificate, or in their absence, Provisional Certificates issued by
          the competent authority. (Mark list will not be accepted in lieu of
          the above).
(iii)     In the case of candidates who have passed S.S.L.C or equivalent
          Certificate, the relevant page of S.S.L.C or equivalent Certificate
          containing the necessary endorsement of eligibility for College
          Admission/Public Service. The pass in Standard X equivalency
          course (‘A’ level) conducted by the Kerala State Literacy Mission
          will be treated as equivalent to SSLC qualification as per G.O.(P)
          No. 21/2015/P&ARD dated 29.06.2015.
(iv)      In the case of candidates with educational qualifications lower than
          those mentioned above, the extract of the relevant portion of the
          school record issued under the signature of the Head of the
          Institution.
Note:- Those who have appeared for the S.S.L.C Examination as Private
       over aged candidates and failed in the Examination will not be
       considered to be possessing any general educational qualification
       on that ground alone. If such candidates have attended any regular
       school course and passed any Standard/Form previously, they will
       be treated to have acquired only a pass in that particular
       Standard/Form as their qualification. [G.O.(Ms) No. 93/79/G.Edn.
       dated 08.06.1979]
(c) Proof of Caste/Community:
  In the case of candidates belonging to Scheduled Castes, Scheduled
  Tribes and Other Backward Classes, who are eligible and have claimed
  the benefit of reservation in appointments or relaxation in upper age
  limit in their profile at the time of making the application on the basis of
  caste/community shall have to produce the following certificates as
  proof of community as and when required by the Commission.
  (1) Caste/Community certificate
       (i) Candidates belonging to Scheduled Castes and Scheduled Tribes
       who are eligible for reservation in appointments and relaxation in
       upper age limit in selection conducted by the Public Service
       Commission shall produce the Caste/Community Certificate, in the
       prescribed format, from the Revenue authorities not below the rank
       of Tahsildar where they reside. The Commission can call for the
       original of the certificate to verify the veracity of the same at the
       time of interview or at any stage of selection. A copy of the Caste
       Certificate would be forwarded to the appointing authority along
       with the advice letter. At the time of joining duty the candidate has
       to produce the original caste certificate before the appointing
       authority for verification.
       (ii) The Caste/Community certificate which has not completed
       three years on the date of Gazette in which the notification of a
       post is published or the Caste certificates issued till the date of
               certificate verification of the post concerned, shall be produced by
               the candidates as and when required by the Commission.
               (iii) The Caste/Community certificates issued online through e-
               District facility will also be accepted.
       Note:- (1) The format prescribed in section 4(1) of Kerala (Scheduled
Castes and Scheduled Tribes) Regulation of Issue of Community Certificate
Rules, 2002 and issued by the Revenue authorities following the guidelines issued
by the Government vide Circular No. 29280/G1/2012/SCSTDD dated 01.02.2013
to the candidates belonging to Scheduled Castes and Schedules Tribes is given in
Appendix IV.
               (2) The list of Scheduled Castes and Scheduled Tribes in the Kerala
               State is given in List I and List II of the Schedule appended to this
               notification.
          (2) Non-Creamy Layer Certificate (NCLC)
               (i) Candidates belonging to Backward Classes entitled to the
               benefit of reservation in appointment shall produce Non-Creamy
               Layer Certificate (NCLC) in the format prescribed and issued by
               competent       authorities   as   laid    down   in   G.O.(P)   No.
               81/2009/SCSTDD dated 26.09.2009, as and when required by the
               Commission. See Appendix V for the format of Non-Creamy Layer
               Certificate.
               (ii) Candidates of the Backward Classes who belong to the group
               of person with hereditary occupation and sub castes of Fishermen
               Community as enumerated in Annexure B and C of the G.O.(P)
               No. 81/2009/SC/STDD dated 26.09.2009 are excluded from the
               Creamy Layer and therefore they need not produce Non-Creamy
               Layer Certificate. However, such candidates will have to produce
               Community Certificate which clearly indicates that they belong
               either to the group of persons with hereditary occupations or sub
               castes of Fishermen Community as and when required by the
               Commission.
       (iii) The Non-Creamy Layer Certificates issued subject to the
       conditions laid down in Kerala Information Technology (Electronic
       Delivery of Services) Rules, 2010 and online through e-District
       facility, in the format prescribed in G.O.(Rt) No. 3942/2018/RD
       dated 24.09.2018 will also be accepted.
       (iv) The Non-Creamy Layer Certificate which has not completed
       one year on the date of Gazette in which the notification of a post
       is published or the Non-Creamy Layer Certificate issued till the
       date of certificate verification of the post concerned, shall be
       produced by the candidates as and when required by the
       Commission.
       (v) The candidates belongs to Other Backward Communities who
       have claimed the benefit of community reservation in appointments
       and relaxation in upper age limit, but not eligible for obtaining
       Non-Creamy Layer Certificate will be given age relaxation, if their
       community is correctly entered in the SSLC as per G.O.(Ms) No.
       1/2021/PIE&MD dated 07.10.2021. School records from States
       other than Kerala will not be accepted as sufficient proof for age
       relaxation.
      Note:- (1) The list of Other Backward Classes in the Kerala State is
              given in List III of the Schedule appended to this
              notification.
              (2) Candidates who are included in the caste/communities
              eligible for reservation, if, either does not enter the
              caste/community in the relevant column of their profile or
              mentions therein a community not included in the list of
              approved communities or enter ‘no' in the profile, he/she
              will forfeit the benefit of community. [G.O.(Ms) No.
              396/10/GAD dated 13.12.2010]
(d) Certificate to be produced by categories not belonging to Scheduled
Castes, Scheduled Tribes or Other Backward Classes of reservation
Communities, but belonging to Economically Weaker Sections (EWS)
(i) Candidates not belonging to Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes or
Other Backward Classes of reservation Communities, but belonging to
Economically Weaker Sections should produce the certificates in the
formats mentioned in Appendix VI, VII, VIII , IX (whichever is
applicable), as and when required by the Commission, for availing the
benefit of reservation. [G.O.(Ms) No. 23/2022/P&ARD dated 04.10.2022,
G.O.(Ms) No. 23/2022/P&EA dated 18.10.2022]
(ii) The EWS Certificate which has not completed one year on the last date
of receipt of application stipulated in the Gazette in which the notification
of a post is published or the EWS Certificate issued till the date of
certificate verification of the post concerned, shall be produced by the
candidates as and when required by the Commission.
(e) Conversion certificate
Candidates who have embraced the Hindu faith by conversion from
Christian Communities shall produce conversion certificates, in original,
issued under the hand and seal of the Secretary of any of the following
institutions, as and when required by the Commission.
       1. Kerala Hindu Mission, Thiruvananthapuram
       2. All India Dayananda Salvation Mission (Arya Samaj), Kerala
       Branch, Thiruvananthapuram
       3. Akhila Bharatha Ayyappa Seva Sangham (Registered as No.22
       of 1964 under the Charitable Societies Act 12 of 1955).
       4.    Shri    Ramadasa      Mission     Universal    Society,    Sree
       Neelakantapuram, Thiruvananthapuram 695581.
       5. Calicut Arya Samaj, Arya Samaj Mandir, Puthiyara P.O., Calicut
– 673004


[G.O.(Ms) No. 174/71/PD dated 15.06.1971, G.O.(Rt) No. 1519/78/DD
dated 09.08.1978, G.O.(Ms) No. 478/78/GAD dated 18.10.1978, G.O.(Ms)
No. 73/83/GAD dated 23.03.1983, G.O.(Ms) No. 112/85/GAD dated
19.03.1985]
Note:- 1) A candidate who is converted or reconverted to Hinduism
       through the organisations authorised for the purpose shall publish a
       notification to that effect in the Gazette indicating the change of
       name or signature to render him eligible for the concession granted
       to various Hindu Communities. The original of the said notification
       shall also be produced as and when required by the Commission.
       Candidates who fail to produce the original of the notification will
       not be entitled to the benefit of reservation or relaxation in upper
       age limit. [G.O.(Ms) No. 97/78/DD dated 24.06.1978]
      2) The Scheduled Caste reconvert to Hinduism or his descendent
      will become entitled to be regarded as a member of Scheduled
      Caste converted to Hinduism only if such a convert has been
      accepted by the members of the Caste claimed as one among
      themselves and has thus become a member of that Caste.
      Therefore, the candidate who claims to have been reconverted to
      Hinduism shall, in addition to the Conversion Certificate
      mentioned above, produce as and when required a certificate to
      prove the above fact obtained from the Revenue Authority
      competent to issue Community Certificates as per the direction laid
      down in Government Circular No. 18421/E2/87/ SC/STDD dated
      15-12-1987. It should be clearly stated by the Revenue authority in
      the certificate that it is issued in accordance with the directions
      stipulated in the Government Circular No. 18421/E2/87/SCSTDD
      dated 15-12-1987.
       3) If there is change of name and caste due to conversion, that
      change will be published in the Gazette and the original of the
      Gazette Notification, along with the school records, as and when
      required by the Commission. In such cases correction in the School
      Records is not necessary.
(f) Where there is difference in the Date of Birth, Community, Religion
etc. of candidates as between the school records and those furnished in the
application:-
(i) In case there is any change in date of birth of the applicant in the School
Admission Register or S.S.L.C. Book or other school records from that
furnished in the application or in the documents produced, necessary
corrections in the Admission Register or other school records shall be
made as required under Rule 3 in Chapter VI of the Kerala Education Act
and Rules. Such corrections made in the School Admission Register or
SSLC Book or other School records shall be authenticated by the
Competent Authority. Benefits, if any, as a result of such change will be
available only after such corrections in the school records are made.
(ii) In the case of change in name, religion and community, change except
clerical error, to be carried out after a person leaves the school shall be
ensured by Gazette Notification after obtaining certificates from Tahsildar
of the Taluks or higher Officers above the rank of Tahsildar in the Revenue
Department, of the applicants requesting for issue of the certificate for the
purpose. Copy of the Gazette Notification duly attested shall be attached to
S.S.L.C. /Extract of Admission Register of the individual concerned. No
change in the original entry shall be made. [G.O.(Ms) No. 80/84/GAD
dated 14.03.1984, G.O.(Ms) No. 195/85/GAD dated 24.05.1985].
(iii) In the case of change in name only, an Identification Certificate
obtained from a Gazette Officer shall be attached with the relevant page in
S.S.L.C./ Extract of Admission Register. [G.O.(Ms) No. 80/84/GAD dated
14.03.1984]
Note:- (1) The original Gazette Notification publishing the change of
       caste/community shall be produced as and when required by the
       Commission. The same should also be produced before the
       appointing authority at the time of joining duty.
       (2) The children born out of inter-caste marriage in which either of
       the parents belongs to Scheduled Caste/Scheduled Tribe are
       entitled to the benefits of Scheduled Castes/ Scheduled Tribes on
       the basis of their proper claim in the application and on the
       production of Community Certificate obtained from the Revenue
       Officer not below the rank of Tahsildar. [G.O.(Ms) No.
       109/2008/SCSTDD dated 20.11.2008]


(g) Identity Proof
Candidates shall produce any one of the Photo affixed identity proof
mentioned below in original along with the Admission ticket/ Interview
memo at the time of Written/ OMR/ Online examinations/ Physical
Efficiency Test, Practical Test, Interview etc.
1. Voter Identity Card issued by Election Commission of India
2. Pan Card
3. Passport
4. Driving Licence
5. Photo affixed Pass book issued by Nationalised Banks
6. Identity Cards issued to Government Employees by the Departments/
Institutions concerned.
7. Identity card issued by Social Welfare Department, Government of
Kerala to Differently Abled persons.
8. Discharge Certificate issued to the Ex-servicemen/ photo affixed
Identity Card issued to the Ex-servicemen by the Zila Sainik Welfare
Officer.
9. Conductor Licence issued by the Motor Vehicle Department.
10. Photo affixed pass book issued by Scheduled Banks/ Kerala State Co-
Operative Bank/ District Co-Operative Banks.
11. Photo affixed Identity Card issued by Public Sector Undertakings
(Various Companies/ Corporations/ Boards/Authority) Govt. Autonomous
Institutions to their employees.
12. Photo affixed Identity card issued by various Universities of Kerala
State to their employees.
13. Photo affixed Medical Certificate issued by the Medical Board to
       Differently Abled candidates
       14. Photo affixed Identity Card issued by Bar Council to those who have
       enrolled as Advocates.
       15. Aadhaar card issued by the Central Government.
       16. One Time Verification Certificate issued by the Kerala Public Service
       Commission
       17. E-aadhaar
       18. National Population Register/ Multi Purpose National Identity Card
       19. Identity card issued by the National Health Authority
       20. e-SHRAM card
14.    Original documents in proof of prescribed educational qualifications, date
       of birth, community, experience etc. shall be produced by the candidates at
       the time of certificate verification or at any time as required by the
       Commission. Those who fail to produce the original documents as
       required above will not be admitted to further stages of selection or
       included in the Ranked List, as the case may be. No extension of time will
       be allowed for the production of any documents. The applications of those
       candidates who have claimed the benefit of community in the application
       but not proved by proper proof will not be rejected for that reason only.
       Such applications will be considered for selection without giving the
       benefit of community, if the application is otherwise in order and the
       candidate is included in the main list of the probability list/short list, as the
       case may be.
       Note:- There will be no relaxation on any circumstances to the condition
that candidates themselves shall appear in person at their own expense for
verification of original documents in the place, date and time as scheduled by the
Commission.
15.    Candidates are required to acquaint themselves with the terms and
       conditions laid down in the notification, instructions given in the General
       Conditions      and    Official    website    of    the    Commission        viz.
       www.keralapsc.gov.in before submitting the application. Applications,
      which do not conform to the above are liable to be rejected. They will not
      get an opportunity to rectify any mistake in the application later.
16.   Claims not made in the profile at the time of making an application for the
      post will not be considered for selection of that post under any
      circumstances. Proof submitted without proper claim in the application
      also will not be considered. Any claims made in the profile subsequent to
      the submission of application will be considered for future selections only.
17.   Qualifications prescribed, including experience, for a post shall be one
      acquired by the candidate on or before the last date fixed for receipt of
      applications for that particular post. The date for determining whether a
      candidate does possess a particular qualification including experience shall
      be the last date fixed for receipt of application. Candidates possessing
      qualifications equivalent to those prescribed for a post shall produce, in
      addition to the documents referred to in Para 13(b), sufficient proof
      obtained from a University or any other competent authority to the effect
      that the qualifications possessed by him/her are recognised as equivalent to
      those prescribed for the post. The application of those who failed to
      produce the proof regarding the equivalency of the qualification possessed
      by them with that prescribed in the notification, as and when required by
      the Commission, will be rejected.         In the case of the examination
      conducted by the University or any other competent authority, the
      candidates will be deemed to have passed the examination only with effect
      from the date of official publication of the results of the Examination by
      the University or the concerned authority.
      Note:- The prescribed educational qualifications claimed by the candidates
             shall be deemed to be acquired by them only on the date in which
             the final result of the course has been declared by the University/
             Board concerned. If the fact regarding the acquirement of the
             prescribed educational qualification by a candidate on or before the
             last date for submission of application of a post is not clear from
             the certificate or mark lists concerned, he/she has to produce a
             certificate showing the date of publication of result of that
             qualification from the University/Board concerned, as and when
             required by the Commission.
18.   (i) As stipulated in the Kerala Government Servant's Application for posts
      (Private Employment and Government Service) Amendment Rules, 2021,
      when a Government servant employed in a service under the
      Administrative control of the Government of Kerala while applying for a
      post in another office or Department of the Government of Kerala, in
      response to notification of the Kerala Public Service Commission, shall
      furnish the fact of his employment in the respective column of the
      application and shall produce a service certificate from the Head of Office
      proving his employment at the time of certificate verification by the
      Commission.
      Note:- (1) The above conditions are not applicable to employees
             provisionally appointed under Rule 9(a) (i) of Kerala State and
             Subordinate Service Rules, 1958, i.e. persons provisionally
             appointed through the employment Exchange.
             (2) In the case of those employed under the Government of India or
             other   State   Governments,      letter   of   consent/Non-Objection
             Certificate of the Government concerned must be produced as and
             when required by the Commission.
             (3) Candidates in Government service applying to another post in
             the same department in service quota need not produce service
             certificate at the time of certificate verification. They shall enter
             their employment details in the application. But a candidate in
             Government service applying for a post in another Department
             must produce the service certificate at the time of certificate
             verification.
             (4) In cases where the special rules for a post prescribes a definite
             period of service as a qualification, the candidate should produce a
             service certificate at the time of certificate verification irrespective
             of the fact whether he has applied for a post in the same
             Department or another Department.

      (ii) Person who have one year tenure to retire from Defence force and is
      applying for a post notified by the Kerala Public Service Commission as
      an Ex-servicemen, he/she should produce the Certificate and undertaking
      as shown in Appendix X in lieu of Non Objection Certificate.

19.   (a) Unless otherwise specified, the experience prescribed as qualification
      for any post in Part I of this notification shall be one gained by candidate
      holding temporary or regular appointments in Central or State Government
      Service or in Public Sector Undertaking or Registered Private Sector
      Undertaking after acquiring the basic qualifications prescribed for the post.
      The experience gained as factory workers on daily wages of a permanent
      nature will also be accepted provided the service is continuous and not of a
      casual nature. Experience gained by candidates in the capacities of paid or
      unpaid apprentices, trainees and casual labourers will not be accepted.

      Note:- Registered Private Sector Undertaking means:
             i. Co-operative Societies registered under the Kerala Co-operative
             Societies Act, 1969, Societies registered under the Societies
             Registration Act,1860 or the Travancore-Cochin Literary, Scientific
             and Charitable Societies Registration Act, 1955 or Companies
             registered under the Companies Act, 1956 or any Institution, Firm
             or Company which has a legal entity under any law for the time
             being in force.
             ii. Small Scale Industrial Units registered with the Industrial
             Development Commissioner; and
             iii. Industrial Institutions wherein Government have investments.

      (b) As per G.O.(Ms) No. 635/2013/H.Edn. dated 05.10.2013, the
      proficiency certificate issued from the Board of Apprenticeship Training
      (BOAT), Ministry of Human Resource Development (MHRD) after the
      completion of one year apprenticeship training will be considered as one
      year experience for employment in State Government Organisations.

20.   In cases where experience is prescribed as a qualification in the selection
      notification, candidates should produce an experience certificate in the
      format given in Appendix XI to prove their experience, as and when
      required by the Commission.
21.   The Ex-servicemen candidates should produce an experience certificate in
      the format given in Appendix XII to prove their experience in the Defence
      Forces. The certificates shall be one issued by competent authority in the
      Defence Force.
22.   Under no circumstances, candidate shall register more than once as per
      “ONE TIME REGISTRATION”. It is the responsibility of candidates to
      ensure the accuracy and correctness of the information uploaded. False
      information should not be furnished. Those who register more than once or
      furnish bogus information are liable to be debarred from applying either
      permanently or for a fixed period or face legal action initiated by the
      Commission.
23.   Unless other methods are instructed, applications must be submitted online
      through the Official website of the Commission www.keralapsc.gov.in
      subsequent to “ONE TIME REGISTRATION”. Candidates who have
      already registered can apply through their profile.
24.   (a) If there is difference in the name of the candidates given in the
      application and that given in the documents, an identity certificate from a
      Gazetted Officer showing that the name given in the application and in the
      documents are of the same person shall be produced as and when required
      by the Commission.
      (b) The application having one or more of the following defects will be
      summarily rejected:
      1. Not qualified.
      2. Under-aged
      3. Over-aged
      4. Applications received not in response to Gazette Notification
      5. Applications devoid of name of the applicant or date of taken the
      photograph or both not imprinted in the photograph
      6. Applications devoid of signature of the applicant or having any other
      image uploaded in the field for uploading the signature

      7. If the candidates apply to more than one District (For District-wise
      selections) for a post in response to the same notification.

      Note:- There will not be any appeal against the rejection of applications
      made on any of the above ground.

25.   Advice on recruitment of candidates will be made on the basis of the rank
      secured by them in the ranked list prepared pursuant to this notification in
      accordance with the rules of reservation prescribed in Rule 14 to 17 of Part
      II of the Kerala State and subordinate Service Rules, 1958 and subject to
      the conditions laid down in Rule 3(c) of the said rules and in accordance
      with the rules made for the purpose from time to time.

      Note:- Rule 3(c) of Part II of the Kerala State and Subordinate Service
      Rules,1958     will not be applicable in respect of candidates for
      appointment in the     Government owned Companies, Corporations and
      Boards, Co-operative Institutions. But in their case the rules laid down in
      this regard by the Company/Corporation/Board/Co-operative Institutions
      will be applicable.

26.   (a) The claim of the candidates with regard to the date of birth,
      educational/technical qualifications, experience and community are
      accepted only provisionally on the information furnished by them in their
      profile at the time of making the application until the Commission are
      satisfied themselves of the claims. Mere admission to any test or interview
      or inclusion of the name of a candidate in a Ranked List will not confer on
      the candidate any right for appointment. The candidature is therefore,
      provisional at all stages of selection and the Commission reserve the right
      to reject candidature at any stage of the selection even after the advice has
      been made.
      (b) Inclusion of the name of an applicant in a Ranked List or in an Advice
      List by the Commission carries with it no guarantee for actual
      appointment.

      (c) The candidates included in the Ranked lists can relinquishment their
      claim for advice for appointment, subject to the conditions laid down in
      Rule 18(ii) of Kerala Public Service Commission Rules of Procedure.
      Candidates shall download the application from the official website of the
      Commission, duly filled in and attested by a Gazetted Officer and submit
      the same, along with a copy of any one of the ID proof approved by the
      Commission and the original of the notarized affidavit, to the concerned
      office of the Commission from where the advice of the post are issued.
      Applications submitted not in the prescribed format and not in consonance
      with the instructions issued by the Commission will be rejected. For more
      instructions in this regard, see the note given in the Ranked list concerned.
      The format of application for relinquishment of advice for appointment is
      given in Appendix XIII.

27.   If written/ OMR/ Online Test is conducted as part of selection of any post,
      candidates shall submit a confirmation for writing the examination through
      their One Time Registration profile. Such candidates alone can generate
      and download the Admission Tickets in the last 15 days till the date of test.
      The applications of candidates who do not submit confirmation within the
      stipulated period will be rejected absolutely. The periods regarding the
      submission of confirmation and the availability of Admission Tickets will
      be published in the Examination Calendar itself. Information in this regard
      will be given to the candidates in their respective profiles and in the
      mobile phone number registered in it.

28.   Admission tickets of candidates will not be send through post. The
      admission ticket of eligible candidates will be made available in their
      profiles. Candidates can download Admission Ticket online by login to
      their profile using their user ID and Password.


29.   Candidates who include their Mobile number/e-mail ID in the profile will
      be informed about Test/Interview through their Mobile phone/e-mail.

30.   Warning:- Candidates who are found guilty of the following items of
      misconduct are liable to be disqualified for being considered for a
      particular post or debarment from applying to the Commission either
      permanently or for any period or the invalidation of their answer scripts or
      products in a written/practical test or the initiations of legal or other
      proceedings against them or their removal or dismissal from the office or
      the ordering of any other legal/disciplinary action against them if they
      have been appointed, or any one or more of the above.
      i. Any attempt by or on behalf of candidates applying for selection by the
      Commission to influence the Chairman or any Member of the Commission
      or the Experts or Examiners who assist the Commission or to canvass
      support for their selection.
      ii. Any attempt to influence the Staff of the Office of the Commission to do
      any undue favour to them or to divulge any information which is to be
      kept secret until it is officially released under the orders of the
      Commission.
      iii. Making of any false statement in the application form or any document
      produced in connection with selection or suppression of any material fact
      relevant to the selection from the Commission.

      iv. Production of any false or tampered document before the Commission
      or the tampering with any document produced before the Commission in
      connection with a selection after their production.
      v. Any attempt to prefer any false complaint before the Commission about
      a rival candidate in a selection.
vi. Making any false allegation against the Chairman or any other Member
of the Commission or any Expert or Examiner who assist the Commission
or any Member of the Staff of the Commission in connection with a
selection.

vii. Any improper behaviour in connection with any interview or
examination held by the Commission.

viii. Tampering with any answer book in an examination conducted by the
Commission or writing anything in such answer book in any manner
which in the opinion of the Commission would lead to the identification of
the candidate against the instructions contained in the answer book.

ix. Any other conduct which in the opinion of the Commission, is likely to
affect the fair and just selection of candidates by the Commission.

x. Electronic gadgets such as Mobile Phones, Blue tooth, Walkman etc. are
prohibited from being used inside the examination halls. Candidates who
possess such gadgets inside the examination halls are liable for
punishment including invalidation of answer scripts by the Commission.

xi.    Disciplinary action as per Rule 22 of Kerala Public Service
Commission, Rules of Procedure will be initiated against those candidates
who after giving confirmation had appeared or failed to appear for the
examination after submitting the application with false claims regarding
their educational qualifications, experience etc.
* xii. Indulging in propaganda through any media including social media
by a candidate who has submitted an application in a selection conducted
by the Kerala Public Service Commission , or by any other person on
his/her behalf in a manner that adversely affects the fair and just selection
of candidates by the Commission .
                                            SAJU GEORGE
                                                 Secretary
                                   Kerala Public Service Commission
                                            Thiruvananthapuram
